# Mr. Robot S04E07 - Act Four picture-only visual evidence v0.1

## A. Source and applicability audit

- Evidence ID: `MRR-S04E07-ACT-FOUR-VISUAL-001`
- Work / season / episode: *Mr. Robot*, S04E07; exact identity is filename/context-supplied, not picture-proven.
- Local media alias (media not committed): `Mr Robot S04E07 local MP4`; raw release/source labels are intentionally excluded.
- Video track: H.264/AVC, `1920x1080`, progressive, `24000/1001 fps`; full file duration `00:56:13.500`.
- Container audio track: AAC 5.1 is present, but audible content was not directly auditioned.
- Continuous scene: YES as an accepted visible single-room/Act-Four edit envelope; uninterrupted story time, production takes and hidden/subframe joins remain UNKNOWN.
- Original dialogue and ambience preserved: UNKNOWN.
- Accepted analysis envelope: `F46433 / 00:32:16.643` inclusive to `F70248 / 00:48:49.927` end-exclusive.
- Frame-exact duration: `23815 frames / 993.283958s`.
- Complete visible-shot count: `201`; manually accepted visible cut boundaries: `200`.
- Locator correction: the supplied `~00:34:40-00:49:45` locator starts inside `S027` and crosses the natural fade/black boundary toward later material; it is not the accepted scene envelope.
- Why this scene tests the selected problem: it provides a single-room multi-body comparison against the two-person contained-space candidates in *Unbelievable* S01E02, especially relation registration, partial-body anchors, object/body continuity and late isolation. It cannot picture-prove coercion, memory, identity, psychology, roles or dialogue.
- Suitability: `PARTIAL` for transferable directing grammar; `STRONG` for picture/edit geometry and rhythm; `UNSUITABLE` for semantic/ethical/psychological claims without a locked original script.

### Accepted boundary and cut method

- `ACCEPTED START`: `F46432` remains in the preceding black/title-card interval; title text is not used as shot evidence. `F46433 / 00:32:16.643` is the first picture frame of P-A.
- `ACCEPTED END`: `F70247 / 00:48:49.885` is the last retained near-black encoded fade-tail frame; `F70248 / 00:48:49.927` is the accepted first visually pure-black boundary after the final fade and is end-exclusive.
- The edit envelope stays in one visibly recurring library-like room. Chair, green-sofa, bookshelf, rear-window and aisle anchors recur. Story-time continuity and unseen entrances/exits remain UNKNOWN.
- Inclusive navigation pass: `368` ffmpeg-scene candidates. Every candidate transition was reviewed as full-picture `F-1 -> F`; only discrete picture discontinuities were accepted.
- Independent all-frame pass: consecutive-frame metrics covered all `23815` selected frames at low resolution with the lower subtitle band excluded from detection. It surfaced `45` additional anomaly clusters; every one was manually reviewed and rejected as same-composition body/camera motion, luminance/exposure change or final fade, not an additional cut.
- Subtitle changes were never accepted as cut or semantic evidence. Detection crops were navigation only; shot description and cut acceptance used full picture.
- Repeated within-shot brightness/exposure changes are OBSERVED in the clip. Their source - diegetic light, edit/grade, encoding or another cause - is UNKNOWN.
- Hidden joins, subframe transitions, production method, camera rig, exact lens/focus method and take count remain UNKNOWN.
- Method status: `ACCEPTED FOR PICTURE/EDIT EVIDENCE`; this is not human creative approval, generation authorization or proof of audience effect.

## B. Scene dramatic structure

- Start state - OBSERVED (`S001-S003 / 00:32:16.643-00:32:51.469`): P-A is seated with a pale patterned cloth crossing the mouth in S001. S002 moves from a high/top-down room view to an elevated wide as P-C rises near P-A and multiple bodies become legible. By S003, P-A's mouth is visibly unobstructed. The exact transition/removal action is not legible at the reviewed wide scale.
- Character objectives - UNKNOWN: gaze, mouth, hands and posture change, but picture alone does not establish anyone's goal, topic, role, permission state, threat, cooperation or refusal.
- Obstacle - UNKNOWN: neither the cloth nor body proximity proves restraint, coercion, trauma, memory, danger, identity conflict or psychological state.
- Event point - OBSERVED only as picture state (`S001-S003`): mouth-area object present -> high/wide multi-body re-registration -> mouth area unobstructed. Remover, force, purpose and causal meaning UNKNOWN.
- Reaction point - UNKNOWN: face/body changes occur throughout, but sound/dialogue were not auditioned and no visible change can be assigned to a known stimulus.
- Turn - INFERRED only as coverage regime: the edit shifts from room/body registration to dense A/B/C/D alternation, then to a late A/B-dominant clean-single run with much longer P-B holds. This does not prove a psychological or power turn.
- End state - OBSERVED (`S188-S201 / 00:44:56.861-00:48:49.927`): clean A/B singles dominate, P-C appears once in S196, P-B receives several long holds, and S201 fades to pure black.
- POV - INFERRED: objective editorial/coverage alignment rather than verified optical POV. No shot proves memory, hallucination, identity or subjective perception.
- Audience information - INFERRED: viewers can track recurring picture sides, room anchors, body positions and folder/page states. Character knowledge and spoken information remain UNKNOWN.

A distant/occluded dark body in the first wide cannot be safely equated to the later P-D. Therefore visible body count is not treated as proof of semantic participant count, acknowledgment, identity or ontology.

### Geometry and Director IR continuity keys

- OBSERVED: P-A's cool-wall/chair side and P-B's bookshelf/green-sofa side recur across many clean singles.
- OBSERVED: P-C's pale sleeveless top/ochre trousers and P-D's glasses/dark jacket form intermittent extra-body edge/depth anchors in A/B views.
- OBSERVED: room wides at S002, S022-S023, S026, S030, S032, S046, S048, S052, S058 and S149 restore some or all chair/sofa/window/aisle geometry.
- OBSERVED: in S181, P-D lowers/moves from the central readable plane while P-C becomes centered/readable in the delivered image. Camera pan/tilt/track, support and focus contribution are UNKNOWN.
- INFERRED: the broad A/B screen-side relation and repeating background anchors support a reusable continuity plan. Exact 180-degree axis, eyeline intent, lens package, camera height and floor-plan dimensions remain UNKNOWN.
- Director IR keys for an original project should include `person_id`, `zone_id`, seat/background side, foreground-person owner, folder/page state version, visible body count, start/end blocking, camera start/path/end and explicit fallback. None of these keys grants generation authorization.

## E. Editing and rhythm

- `201` shots / `200` visible cuts across `993.283958s`.
- Mean shot duration: `4.942s`; median: `3.128s`; Q1: `2.085s`; Q3: `5.088s`.
- Minimum: `S027`, `0.417083s`; maximum: `S194`, `67.400667s`.
- Duration bins: `<1s` 7, `1-<2s` 39, `2-<4s` 83, `4-<8s` 51, `8-<16s` 13, `16-<32s` 5, `>=32s` 3.
- Single-clip pattern: dense mid-envelope alternation peaks in P3 (`64` shots over `198.407s`; mean `3.100s`), while P6 slows to `13` shots over `201.785s`; mean `15.522s`, median `8.050s`.
- Long single occurrences: S002 `28.362s` high-to-wide room reveal; S173 `38.705s` P-B; S194 `67.401s` P-B; S197 `42.042s` P-B; S199 `20.270s` P-B; S201 `31.281s` P-B/fade. These durations describe this clip only and are not prescriptions.
- Repeated short cuts and brightness changes were not conflated: seven shots are under one second, while repeated within-shot luminance/exposure events were manually rejected as cuts.

## F. Sound and performance grammar

- `SND-UNK` for every shot: dialogue, ambience, score, silence, overlap, off-screen cues, breaths, page sounds, sound bridges and sound-led cuts were not directly auditioned.
- Burned subtitles were excluded from semantic evidence. They cannot prove who speaks, what a face responds to, an identity, memory, coercion, role, relationship or any shot fact.
- OBSERVED performance evidence is limited to visible eye, head, mouth, hand, posture, body-route and object-state changes. Emotion and reaction cause remain UNKNOWN.
- OBSERVED long-hold progressions: P-B changes head/gaze/mouth and, where visible, hand state inside S173, S194, S197, S199 and S201. The package does not label those changes as fear, grief, confession, recognition or any other psychology.
- OBSERVED object/body action: P-C separates a pale sheet from the green folder in S076; P-C moves into and away from P-A's frame in S087-S090; exact text, purpose, permission and force remain UNKNOWN.

## G. Single-source transfer candidates

All four entries are `HYPOTHESIS / candidate rule`, not promoted grammar. Their trigger must come from a locked original script. The *Unbelievable* S01E02 comparison is a local evidence boundary, not proof that either source shares the same ethical, conversational or dramatic trigger.

## H. Evidence boundary

### VERIFIED FACT / OBSERVED

- Exact accepted picture envelope, `201` complete shots, `200` visible cut boundaries and all frame/time ranges in the TSV.
- Recurring room anchors, visible body/wardrobe aliases, body routes, the opening mouth-area cloth state, green-folder/pale-page appearances, S076 sheet separation, and the S181 change from P-D central readability to P-C central readability; camera-movement/focus contribution is UNKNOWN. Late clean-single pattern and final fade are also visible.
- Repeated luminance/exposure changes occur within unchanged compositions; they are not counted as cuts.

### EVIDENCE-BASED INFERENCE

- Objective editorial coverage, geometry-refresh functions, screen-ownership changes, a broad A/B relation axis and the phase labels.
- The comparison to *Unbelievable* concerns only visible container/anchor/reset mechanisms, not shared story meaning.

### HYPOTHESIS

- The four transfer candidates in Section G. They require a locked original-script trigger, original project geometry/assets, a contrary test and human review before grammar promotion.

### UNKNOWN / prohibited claims

- Dialogue, speaker/turn order, ambience, score, silence, sound bridges and reaction cues.
- Coercion, restraint, consent, threat, safety, trauma, memory, confession, recognition, identity, psychology, hallucination, ontology, authority, profession, relationship, intention and objective.
- Whether P-D is acknowledged by any person or shares the same ontological status as any other visible body.
- Mouth-cloth material/purpose/removal/force; folder/page text, ownership, identity and meaning; off-frame entry/exit/action; story time; hidden joins; production method; camera rig/lens/focus support.

## Do-not-copy boundary

- Do not copy characters, identities, wardrobe, pale mouth cloth, green folder/pages, library room, green sofa, rear windows, body placements, signature compositions, exact scene events, subtitle/dialogue content, shot order or source durations into generation instructions.
- Transfer only abstract decision logic to project-original people, spaces, objects and locked-script facts.
- Source media, frames, contact sheets, subtitles and audio remain in a local temporary review directory and must never be committed.
